  try {
    if (window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone) return;
    window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone = true;

    var path = (window.location.pathname || '/').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

/* ============================================================
   Collapsible right table of contents (toggled by toc-collapse.js)
   ============================================================ */

/* Dimensions mirrored from Mintlify's compiled mint-theme layout. If a
   Mintlify update moves the button or the collapsed reading column,
   re-measure these on a rendered page. */
:root {
  --toc-wrapper-max: 92rem; /* centered layout wrapper max-width */
  --toc-wrapper-pad: 2rem; /* wrapper right padding */
  --toc-col: 19rem; /* ToC column width, incl. its 2.5rem left padding */
  --toc-side: 28rem; /* side column space #content-area reserves */
  --toc-btn-size: 1.75rem; /* ours, not mirrored: the toggle's diameter */
}

/* Hidden by default: below the xl breakpoint Mintlify hides the ToC
   anyway, and pages that render no ToC (wide-mode pages) get no toggle. */
#toc-collapse-toggle {
  display: none;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  position: fixed;
  top: 10rem;
  /* Sits just left of the ToC column, inside its 2.5rem left-padding
     strip: at the column's left edge, minus the button's own width and
     a small gap. 100% rather than 100vw so a classic scrollbar doesn't
     shift the math (percentages on fixed elements resolve against the
     layout viewport). */
  right: calc(
    max((100% - var(--toc-wrapper-max)) / 2, 0px) + var(--toc-wrapper-pad) +
      var(--toc-col) - var(--toc-btn-size) - 0.35rem
  );
  transition: right 200ms ease-out;
  z-index: 30;
  width: var(--toc-btn-size);
  height: var(--toc-btn-size);
  border-radius: 9999px;
  border: 1px solid hsl(var(--border-300) / 0.15);
  background: hsl(var(--bg-100));
  color: hsl(var(--text-400));
  cursor: pointer;
}

#toc-collapse-toggle:hover {
  background: hsl(var(--bg-300));
  color: hsl(var(--text-100));
}

#toc-collapse-toggle svg {
  width: 14px;
  height: 14px;
  fill: none;
  stroke: currentColor;
  stroke-width: 2.5;
  stroke-linecap: round;
  stroke-linejoin: round;
}

/* The list glyph only appears while the button is parked at the right
   edge, so the collapsed control still reads as the table of contents. */
#toc-collapse-toggle .toc-glyph {
  display: none;
  stroke-width: 2;
}

/* A fixed-position button would repeat on every printed page. */
@media print {
  #toc-collapse-toggle {
    display: none !important;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  #toc-collapse-toggle {
    transition: none;
  }
}

@media (min-width: 1280px) {
  html:has(#table-of-contents-layout) #toc-collapse-toggle {
    display: flex;
  }

  html.toc-collapsed #toc-collapse-toggle .toc-chevron {
    transform: rotate(180deg);
  }

  /* The column is gone while collapsed, so the button moves to the
     right edge where the restored ToC will reappear, widening into a
     pill that pairs the chevron with the list glyph. */
  html.toc-collapsed #toc-collapse-toggle {
    right: 0.5rem;
    width: auto;
    padding: 0 0.5rem;
    gap: 0.25rem;
  }

  html.toc-collapsed #toc-collapse-toggle .toc-glyph {
    display: block;
  }

  html.toc-collapsed #table-of-contents-layout {
    display: none !important;
  }

  /* Also hide the side column's wrapper: left in flow at zero width,
     it keeps its 3rem flex gap as dead space at the right edge. Scoped
     with :has() so the wrapper is untouched when it holds other
     content, such as the open assistant panel. */
  html.toc-collapsed #content-side-layout:has(#table-of-contents-layout) {
    display: none !important;
  }

  /* Mintlify sizes the content column assuming the side column is
     present (xl:w-[calc(100%-28rem)]); reclaim that space when collapsed.
     Scoped with :has() so pages that render no ToC, such as wide-mode
     pages, keep their own layout. Written out twice rather than nested:
     CSS nesting shipped later than :has(), so nesting here would break
     the cap in browsers that pass the :has() support guard. */
  html.toc-collapsed #content-container:has(#table-of-contents-layout) #content-area {
    width: 100% !important;
  }

  /* Safety bound on the widened reading column. The centered wrapper
     already caps overall width, so today this only bites at the
     wrapper's max, where it equals the natural content width; it exists
     so line length can't run away if the wrapper cap ever changes. */
  html.toc-collapsed #content-container:has(#table-of-contents-layout) #content-area > * {
    max-width: calc(var(--toc-wrapper-max) - var(--toc-side));
  }
}

@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .digest-feature {
    padding: 1.25rem 1rem 1rem;
    margin: 1.5rem 0;
  }
  #content-container .digest-feature-title {
    font-size: 1.375rem;
  }
  .digest-wins {
    padding: 1.25rem 1rem 1rem;
  }
  .digest-wins-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  #content-container .digest-wins-grid > div:nth-child(2) {
    border-top: 1px solid hsl(var(--border-300) / 0.06);
    padding-top: 0.5rem;
  }
  #content-container .digest-wins-grid > div:nth-child(2)::before {
    top: 0.5rem;
  }
}

stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></button></div></header><div class="mdx-content @container/columns-container relative mt-8 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="How Claude Code uses prompt caching" data-page-href="/en/prompt-caching" id="content"><span data-as="p">Prompt caching makes Claude Code faster and more cost-efficient. Without caching, the API would reprocess your full history on every turn. With caching, it reuses what it already processed, bills the re-read at the <a href="https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/about-claude/pricing" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">cached token rate</a>, and fully processes only what changed.</span>
<span data-as="p">Claude Code handles prompt caching for you, unless you <a href="#disable-prompt-caching" class="link">disable it</a>. It is still useful to know how prompt caching works, because some actions invalidate the cache and make the next response slower and more expensive while it rebuilds. This page covers which actions those are, why some settings wait for a restart to apply, and how to check cache performance when usage looks high.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="how-the-cache-is-organized"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#how-the-cache-is-organized" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">How the cache is organized</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Each time you send a message in Claude Code, it makes a new API request. The model doesn’t remember anything between requests, so Claude Code re-sends the full context: the system prompt, your project context, every prior message and tool result, and your new message. New content is appended at the end, which means most of each request is identical to the one before it. Prompt caching is how the API avoids reprocessing the part that didn’t change.</span>
<span data-as="p">The API caches by matching the start of each request, called the prefix, against content it recently processed. On a normal turn, the prefix is the entire previous request and only the latest exchange is new. The match is exact, so a change anywhere in the prefix recomputes everything after it. There is no per-file or per-segment caching. See <a href="https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/prompt-caching#how-prompt-caching-works" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">how prompt caching works</a> in the API reference for the underlying mechanism.</span>

15.25L7.25 10.75" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></button>
<span data-as="p">To get the most out of prefix matching, Claude Code orders each request so content that rarely changes between turns comes first:</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_24llktbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Layer</th><th>Content</th><th>Changes when</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>System prompt</td><td>Core instructions, tool definitions</td><td>The set of loaded tool definitions changes</td></tr><tr><td>Project context</td><td>CLAUDE.md, auto memory, unscoped rules</td><td>Session starts, or after <code>/clear</code> or <code>/compact</code></td></tr><tr><td>Conversation</td><td>Your messages, Claude’s responses, tool results</td><td>Every turn</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">A change to the conversation layer leaves the system prompt and project context cached. A change to the system prompt invalidates everything, because all later content now sits behind a different prefix. The third column gives common triggers rather than an exhaustive list, and the sections below cover the full set.</span>
<span data-as="p">The prefix-match rule explains most of the behaviors on this page. <a class="link" href="/docs/en/permission-modes#analyze-before-you-edit-with-plan-mode">Plan mode</a> and <a class="link" href="/docs/en/skills">skill loading</a>, for example, append their instructions as conversation messages, so the cached prefix stays intact.</span>
<span data-as="p">Two settings don’t appear in the layer table but still affect what stays cached:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model</strong>: each model has its own cache. Switching models recomputes the entire request even when the content is identical. See <a href="#switching-models" class="link">Switching models</a> below.</li>
<li><strong>Effort level</strong>: on most models, each effort level has its own cache, so changing effort mid-session recomputes the entire request. On Opus 5.5 and Fable 5.1 with an API key or a Claude subscription, the cache stays intact by default. See <a href="#changing-effort-level" class="link">Changing effort level</a> below.</li>
</ul>
<div role="note" aria-label="Tip" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-green-200 bg-green-50 dark:border-green-900 dark:bg-green-600/20 [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-green-800 dark:text-green-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">Pick your model and effort level at the top of a session, then save <code>/compact</code> for natural breaks between tasks. The fewer changes you make mid-task, the higher your cache hit rate.</span></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="where-the-cache-lives"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#where-the-cache-lives" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Where the cache lives</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Caching happens server-side, in whichever infrastructure serves your model. Where that is depends on how you authenticate:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>API key, Claude subscription, or <a class="link" href="/docs/en/claude-platform-on-aws">Claude Platform on AWS</a></strong>: the cache lives in Anthropic’s infrastructure, accessed through the <a href="https://platform.claude.com/docs" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Claude API</a></li>
<li><strong>Amazon Bedrock or Google Cloud’s Agent Platform</strong>: the cache lives in your cloud provider’s serving infrastructure</li>
<li><strong>Microsoft Foundry</strong>: depends on the deployment’s <a href="https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-in-microsoft-foundry#hosting-options" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">hosting option</a>. Hosted on Azure deployments are served on Azure infrastructure; Hosted on Anthropic deployments are served on Anthropic’s infrastructure</li>
<li><strong>Custom <code>ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL</code> or <a class="link" href="/docs/en/llm-gateway">LLM gateway</a></strong>: the cache lives wherever your requests are forwarded, and whether caching works depends on the gateway</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Claude Code also appends system context mid-conversation, such as file-change notices, and marks that block for caching on every provider and connection unless you set <a class="link" href="/docs/en/llm-gateway-protocol#disable-pre-release-capabilities"><code>CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_BETAS</code></a>, in which case that block is sent uncached.</span>
<span data-as="p">At the provider’s own endpoint, Amazon Bedrock and its <a class="link" href="/docs/en/amazon-bedrock#use-the-mantle-endpoint">Mantle endpoint</a>, Google Cloud’s Agent Platform, and Microsoft Foundry cache the block the same way the Claude API does.</span>
<span data-as="p">When your requests pass through an <a class="link" href="/docs/en/llm-gateway">LLM gateway</a>, a custom <code>ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL</code>, or a cloud provider base-URL override such as <a class="link" href="/docs/en/env-vars"><code>ANTHROPIC_BEDROCK_BASE_URL</code></a>, what stays cached depends on how the gateway handles the <a href="https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/prompt-caching#explicit-cache-breakpoints" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link"><code>cache_control</code> markers</a> Claude Code sends:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Forwards them unchanged</strong>: the block and your conversation cache the same as at the provider’s own endpoint.</li>
<li><strong>Rejects the marked request with a <code>400</code> error naming <code>cache_control</code></strong>: Claude Code re-sends the request with the marker moved off the block and onto your last conversation message, and keeps it there for the rest of the conversation. The block bills as uncached input; your conversation stays cached.</li>
<li><strong>Removes the markers while returning success</strong>: your entire conversation history bills as uncached input on every turn. A gateway that converts block-form system content to a plain string drops the marker the same way.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">For what each provider stores and processes, see <a class="link" href="/docs/en/data-usage">data usage</a>. Wherever the cache lives, entries expire after a period of inactivity, and <a href="#cache-lifetime" class="link">Cache lifetime</a> below covers the TTL and how to extend it.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="actions-that-invalidate-the-cache"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#actions-that-invalidate-the-cache" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Actions that invalidate the cache</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">These actions cause the next request to miss part or all of the cache. You see a one-time slower, more expensive turn, after which the new prefix is cached. Most of them are avoidable mid-task once you know they have a cost. A model switch can feel free until you notice the slower turn that follows.</span>
<ul>
<li><a href="#switching-models" class="link">Switching models</a></li>
<li><a href="#changing-effort-level" class="link">Changing effort level</a></li>
<li><a href="#turning-on-fast-mode" class="link">Turning on fast mode</a></li>
<li><a href="#connecting-or-disconnecting-an-mcp-server" class="link">Connecting or disconnecting an MCP server</a></li>
<li><a href="#enabling-or-disabling-a-plugin" class="link">Enabling or disabling a plugin</a></li>
<li><a href="#denying-an-entire-tool" class="link">Denying an entire tool</a></li>
<li><a href="#compacting-the-conversation" class="link">Compacting the conversation</a></li>
<li><a href="#accumulating-many-images" class="link">Accumulating many images</a></li>
<li><a href="#upgrading-claude-code" class="link">Upgrading Claude Code</a></li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="switching-models"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#switching-models" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Switching models</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Each model has its own cache. Switching with <a class="link" href="/docs/en/model-config#setting-your-model"><code>/model</code></a> means the next request reads the entire conversation history with no cache hits, even though the content is identical.</span>
<span data-as="p">When you run <code>/model</code> at the terminal, Claude Code asks you to confirm the switch only while the cache is still warm and the new model isn’t the one that produced the last response. The cache stays warm for one <a href="#cache-lifetime" class="link">cache TTL</a> after Claude Code last sent a request in this conversation or Claude last responded. Once that time passes, the cache has expired, so Claude Code switches without asking.</span>
<span data-as="p">Before v2.1.238, Claude Code didn’t check the cache TTL and asked even after the cache had expired.</span>
<span data-as="p">You can also require this confirmation or skip it with a <a class="link" href="/docs/en/hooks#premodelswitch-decision-control">PreModelSwitch hook</a>.</span>
<span data-as="p">The <a class="link" href="/docs/en/model-config#opusplan-model-setting"><code>opusplan</code> model setting</a> resolves to Opus during plan mode and Sonnet during execution, so each plan-mode toggle is a model switch and starts a fresh cache.</span>
<span data-as="p"><a class="link" href="/docs/en/model-config#automatic-model-fallback">Automatic model fallback</a> on Fable models, Opus 5.5, and Opus 5 is also a model switch. When a safety classifier flags a request in a category that has a fallback model, Claude Code re-runs the request on that model and the session continues there.</span>
<span data-as="p">When a skill or command’s frontmatter names a <a class="link" href="/docs/en/skills#frontmatter-reference"><code>model</code></a> other than the session’s current model, that turn is also a model switch: the next request reads the entire conversation history with no cache hits. The session model resumes on your next prompt. A <code>context: fork</code> skill sets the <a class="link" href="/docs/en/skills#run-skills-in-a-subagent">forked subagent’s model</a> instead.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="changing-effort-level"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#changing-effort-level" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Changing effort level</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">On most models, changing the <a class="link" href="/docs/en/model-config#adjust-effort-level">effort level</a> mid-session means the next request reads the entire conversation history with no cache hits. While the cache is still warm, Claude Code asks you to confirm the change first.</span>
<span data-as="p">On Opus 5.5 and Fable 5.1 with an API key or a Claude subscription, changing effort keeps the cache, and Claude Code applies the new level without asking. This doesn’t apply on Amazon Bedrock, Google Cloud’s Agent Platform, or a <a class="link" href="/docs/en/claude-apps-gateway">Claude apps gateway</a>, or when you set <a class="link" href="/docs/en/llm-gateway-protocol#disable-pre-release-capabilities"><code>CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_BETAS</code></a> or your organization has a HIPAA configuration.</span>
<span data-as="p">Before v2.1.260, changing effort on Fable 5.1 with an API key or a Claude subscription also invalidated the cache.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="turning-on-fast-mode"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#turning-on-fast-mode" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Turning on fast mode</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Enabling <a class="link" href="/docs/en/fast-mode">fast mode</a> adds a request header that is part of the cache key, so the first request Claude Code sends with fast mode on reads the entire conversation history with no cache hits. Claude Code sets that header once when a turn starts and keeps it for the whole turn, so when you turn fast mode on while Claude is working, the cache miss from the header happens on the first request of your next turn. Those uncached input tokens are billed at <a class="link" href="/docs/en/fast-mode#understand-the-cost-tradeoff">fast mode rates</a>, which is why turning it on at the start of a session costs less than turning it on deep into a long one. If your current model doesn’t support fast mode, enabling fast mode also <a href="#switching-models" class="link">switches your model</a>, and that switch starts a fresh cache on its own from the next request in the running turn.</span>
<span data-as="p">The cost applies once per conversation. After the first fast mode turn, Claude Code keeps sending the header and varies only the request’s speed setting, which is not part of the cache key. Turning fast mode off, the <a class="link" href="/docs/en/fast-mode#handle-rate-limits">automatic fallback to standard speed</a> after a rate limit, and turning it back on later all keep the cache. If you <a class="link" href="/docs/en/fast-mode#handle-rate-limits">run out of usage credits</a> mid-session, Claude Code retries each rejected fast mode request at standard speed the same way, so this fallback also keeps the cache. <code>/clear</code> and <code>/compact</code> reset this, since they rebuild the cache at those points anyway.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="connecting-or-disconnecting-an-mcp-server"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#connecting-or-disconnecting-an-mcp-server" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Connecting or disconnecting an MCP server</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Tool definitions sit in the system prompt layer, so the cache invalidates when the set of tool definitions in the request changes between turns. Toggling the <a class="link" href="/docs/en/advisor">advisor tool</a> is an exception: its definition sits after the cache breakpoint, so enabling or disabling <code>/advisor</code> keeps the cached prefix intact. Whether an <a class="link" href="/docs/en/mcp">MCP server</a> change does this depends on whether its tools are deferred by <a class="link" href="/docs/en/mcp#scale-with-mcp-tool-search">tool search</a> or loaded into the prefix:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Deferred tools</strong>, the default on supported models: a server connecting, disconnecting, or changing its tool list only appends new content and doesn’t disturb anything already cached.</li>
<li><strong>Tools loaded into the prefix</strong>: any change to them invalidates the cache. This happens when <a class="link" href="/docs/en/mcp#configure-tool-search">tool search is unavailable or disabled</a>, such as on Google Cloud’s Agent Platform models earlier than the Claude 4.5 generation, with a custom <code>ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL</code> gateway, or on a Microsoft Foundry <a href="https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-in-microsoft-foundry#hosting-options" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">deployment hosted on Azure</a> once Claude Code detects that the deployment rejects tool search. It also happens for a server or tool marked <a class="link" href="/docs/en/mcp#exempt-a-server-from-deferral"><code>alwaysLoad</code></a>, and for definitions kept upfront by <a class="link" href="/docs/en/mcp#configure-tool-search">threshold-based loading</a>.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">When tools load into the prefix, the most common cause of an invalidation is a server connecting or disconnecting mid-session, which can happen without any action on your part: a stdio server’s process exits, an HTTP session expires, or a server <a class="link" href="/docs/en/mcp#automatic-reconnection">reconnects automatically after a transient failure</a>. A connected server can also push a <a class="link" href="/docs/en/mcp#dynamic-tool-updates">dynamic tool update</a> that changes its tool list.</span>
<span data-as="p">Editing your MCP config does not by itself change the cache. The new config takes effect only after a restart, which is when the server connects or disconnects.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="enabling-or-disabling-a-plugin"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#enabling-or-disabling-a-plugin" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Enabling or disabling a plugin</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">When you enable or disable a <a class="link" href="/docs/en/plugins">plugin</a>, what the change costs depends on which component types the plugin provides. The cases below cover each component type, when Claude Code applies the change, and what happens when you disable a plugin again in the same session.</span>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="plugin-components-that-keep-the-cache"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#plugin-components-that-keep-the-cache" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Plugin components that keep the cache</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">Claude Code never invalidates the cache for a plugin’s skills, commands, agents, hooks, monitors, or themes. It appends their content after the existing conversation, so the next request pays for that content and still reads everything before it from the cache.</span>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="plugins-that-provide-mcp-servers"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#plugins-that-provide-mcp-servers" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Plugins that provide MCP servers</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">When you enable or disable a plugin that provides <a class="link" href="/docs/en/plugins-reference#mcp-servers">MCP servers</a>, Claude Code follows the same rules as when you <a href="#connecting-or-disconnecting-an-mcp-server" class="link">connect or disconnect an MCP server</a>:</span>
<ul>
<li>If Claude Code defers the server’s tools, it keeps the cache.</li>
<li>If Claude Code loads them into the prefix, the next request re-reads the entire conversation.</li>
</ul>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="code-intelligence-plugins"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#code-intelligence-plugins" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Code intelligence plugins</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">When you enable a <a class="link" href="/docs/en/discover-plugins#code-intelligence">code intelligence plugin</a>, Claude gets the <a class="link" href="/docs/en/tools-reference#lsp-tool-behavior">LSP tool</a>.</span>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="when-plugin-changes-apply"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#when-plugin-changes-apply" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">When plugin changes apply</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">A change you make in the <code>/plugin</code> menu goes through <a class="link" href="/docs/en/discover-plugins#apply-plugin-changes-without-restarting"><code>/reload-plugins</code></a>, which Claude Code runs for you when you close the menu. You pay the cost, whether appended announcements or a full re-read, on the first turn after the change applies. Claude Code can also apply a change on its own:</span>
<ul>
<li>For a plugin with a <code>command</code> source, Claude Code <a class="link" href="/docs/en/plugin-marketplaces#when-claude-code-re-runs-the-command">can reload the plugin itself</a>.</li>
<li>When you <a class="link" href="/docs/en/discover-plugins#install-plugins">install a plugin from the <code>/plugin</code> interface</a>, Claude Code can activate it during the install. The install summary tells you whether it did.</li>
<li>When you <a class="link" href="/docs/en/permissions#move-the-session-to-another-directory">move the session with <code>/cd</code></a> on v2.1.246 or later, Claude Code applies the plugins the new directory’s settings enable as part of the move, without the full re-read warning that holds a <code>/reload-plugins</code>.</li>
<li>In interactive sessions, when you add or remove a plugin in a <a class="link" href="/docs/en/plugins#test-your-plugins-locally">folder of plugins</a> you passed with <code>--plugin-dir</code>, the change applies right away. If applying it would trigger a full re-read, Claude Code holds the change instead and shows a notice to run <code>/reload-plugins</code>. Requires Claude Code v2.1.265 or later.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">When <code>/reload-plugins</code> runs and the reload would trigger a full re-read, Claude Code shows a warning and doesn’t apply the reload. Run <code>/reload-plugins --force</code> to apply it anyway.</span>
<span data-as="p"><code>/reload-plugins</code> also runs in sessions without an interactive terminal, such as the desktop app, the Agent SDK, and <a class="link" href="/docs/en/headless">non-interactive mode</a> with <code>-p</code>, when you type it into the session directly. Requires Claude Code v2.1.260 or later.</span>
<span data-as="p">In those sessions the reload applies everything except plugin MCP server changes, which <a class="link" href="/docs/en/discover-plugins#apply-plugin-changes-without-restarting">take effect in your next session</a> and so never cost a full re-read mid-session.</span>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="plugins-you-enable-and-then-disable-in-one-session"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#plugins-you-enable-and-then-disable-in-one-session" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Plugins you enable and then disable in one session</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">When you disable a plugin you enabled earlier in the session, Claude Code restores the previous request shape. If that prefix is still within its <a href="#cache-lifetime" class="link">cache lifetime</a>, the next request reads the older cache entry instead of rebuilding.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="denying-an-entire-tool"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#denying-an-entire-tool" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Denying an entire tool</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">If you add a bare tool name like <code>Bash</code> or <code>WebFetch</code> as a <a class="link" href="/docs/en/permissions#manage-permissions">deny rule</a>, Claude can’t call that tool from your next request on, whether you add the rule through <code>/permissions</code> or by <a class="link" href="/docs/en/settings#when-edits-take-effect">editing a settings file directly</a>. That includes a rule you add through <code>/permissions</code> in the middle of a turn.</span>
<span data-as="p">When <a class="link" href="/docs/en/mcp#scale-with-mcp-tool-search">tool search</a> is active, which is the default on supported models, the request’s tool definitions don’t change and the cached prefix survives. When tool search is unavailable or disabled, Claude Code removes the definition from the next request, which invalidates the cache, and so does removing the rule later.</span>
<span data-as="p">Only a deny rule that matches in the tool-name position blocks a tool this way: a bare tool name, the equivalent <code>Bash(*)</code> form, or a <a class="link" href="/docs/en/permissions#tool-name-wildcards">tool-name glob</a> like <code>&quot;*&quot;</code>. A glob that matches only MCP tools, such as <code>&quot;mcp__*&quot;</code>, blocks those tools the same way. Scoped deny rules like <code>Bash(rm *)</code>, and all allow and ask rules, don’t change which tools Claude sees. Claude Code checks them when Claude attempts a call, leaving the prefix intact.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="compacting-the-conversation"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#compacting-the-conversation" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Compacting the conversation</span></h3>
<span data-as="p"><a class="link" href="/docs/en/context-window#what-survives-compaction">Compaction</a> replaces your message history with a summary. By design, this invalidates the conversation layer, since the next request has a new, shorter history that doesn’t share a prefix with the old one. Claude Code reuses the system prompt layer unless the conversation was <a href="#resuming-a-session" class="link">resumed while keeping a system prompt that would otherwise have changed</a>; in that case the first compaction switches to the current prompt and that layer rebuilds once. It reloads project context from disk, which cache-hits only if CLAUDE.md and memory are unchanged since the session started.</span>
<span data-as="p">To produce the summary, Claude Code sends a separate request with the same system prompt, tools, and history as your conversation, plus a summarization instruction appended as a final user message. While the cache is warm, that request reads your prefix from the cache, so a mid-session <code>/compact</code> costs a fraction of what the context size suggests and spends most of its time generating the summary.</span>
<span data-as="p">After a break longer than the <a href="#cache-lifetime" class="link">cache lifetime</a>, there is no cache left to read, so the summarization request reprocesses the full history as uncached input. This is why <code>/compact</code> costs the most when you <a class="link" href="/docs/en/sessions#resume-from-a-summary">resume an old session</a>. In both the warm and cold cases, the turn after compaction rebuilds the conversation cache for only the much shorter summary, so that turn is not the slow part.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Tip" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-green-200 bg-green-50 dark:border-green-900 dark:bg-green-600/20 [&amp;_[data-component-part=&#x27;callout-icon&#x27;]]:mt-px" data-callout-type="tip"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-4.5 text-green-800 dark:text-green-300"><path d="M 4.0216 15.9727 C 4.0216 16.1941 4.0855 16.4101 4.2074 16.5947 L 4.81 17.4979 C 4.9946 17.7747 5.4137 18 5.7467 18 H 7.9149 C 8.2468 18 8.6659 17.775 8.8505 17.4979 L 9.451 16.5951 C 9.5543 16.439 9.6391 16.1601 9.6391 15.9727 L 9.6436 14.5945 H 4.0181 L 4.0216 15.9727 Z M 6.8308 0 C 3.2453 0.0113 0.6429 2.9171 0.6429 6.1563 C 0.6429 7.7167 1.2209 9.1392 2.1744 10.2278 C 2.7556 10.8901 3.663 12.2751 4.0104 13.443 C 4.0115 13.4518 4.0137 13.4612 4.0148 13.4706 H 9.6473 C 9.6483 13.4612 9.6505 13.4524 9.6517 13.443 C 9.9988 12.2751 10.9065 10.8901 11.4877 10.2278 C 12.4422 9.1695 13.0189 7.749 13.0189 6.1563 C 13.0189 2.7705 10.2483 0.0001 6.8308 0 Z M 10.2202 9.1449 C 9.6696 9.7724 8.9882 10.7727 8.4956 11.8131 H 5.1692 C 4.6766 10.7727 3.9953 9.7724 3.445 9.1452 C 2.7257 8.3257 2.3305 7.2463 2.3305 6.1563 C 2.3305 3.9835 4.0216 1.6964 6.7992 1.6876 C 9.3131 1.6876 11.3312 3.7058 11.3312 6.1563 C 11.3312 7.2463 10.9374 8.3257 10.2202 9.1449 Z M 6.2683 2.8127 C 4.7178 2.8127 3.4556 4.0749 3.4556 5.6254 C 3.4556 5.9364 3.7072 6.188 4.0181 6.188 C 4.3291 6.188 4.5807 5.9348 4.5807 5.6254 C 4.5807 4.6948 5.3376 3.9378 6.2683 3.9378 C 6.5792 3.9378 6.8308 3.6865 6.8308 3.3756 C 6.8308 3.0647 6.5777 2.8127 6.2683 2.8127 Z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-green-800 dark:text-green-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">Compaction works in your favor when the context you discard is content you no longer need. To choose when its overhead happens, run <code>/compact</code> at a natural break in your work, such as between tasks, instead of waiting for auto-compaction to trigger mid-task. If you’ve gone down a path you want to abandon entirely, <a href="#rewinding-the-conversation" class="link"><code>/rewind</code></a> to an earlier turn instead. Rewinding truncates back to a prefix that is already cached, rather than building a new one as compaction does.</span></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="accumulating-many-images"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#accumulating-many-images" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Accumulating many images</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The API limits how many images and PDFs each request can carry. For the current numbers, see <a href="https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/vision#request-limits" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Request limits</a> in the API docs. Claude Code also caps the total size of the images and PDFs in a request, so large screenshots reach the limit with fewer images than small ones.</span>
<span data-as="p">When the next request would pass either limit, Claude Code removes a batch of the oldest images and PDFs from what it sends, which leaves room for more before it needs to remove any again. Claude can no longer see the removed images. If Claude needs one of them again, share it again.</span>
<span data-as="p">Removing images changes the messages that held them, so the next request reprocesses the conversation from the earliest of those messages onward. Because Claude Code removes a batch at a time, you see one slower turn per batch rather than one with each new screenshot.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="upgrading-claude-code"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#upgrading-claude-code" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Upgrading Claude Code</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">A new Claude Code version typically updates the system prompt or tool definitions, so the first conversation you start after an upgrade builds its cache from the top. <a class="link" href="/docs/en/setup#auto-updates">Auto-update</a> downloads new versions in the background but applies them on the next launch, never mid-session, so you see this as an uncached first turn after restarting rather than a surprise during a session. Set <code>DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1</code> to control when upgrades apply.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><path d="M 9 1.6714 C 13.0371 1.6714 16.3286 4.9629 16.3286 9 C 16.3286 13.0371 13.0371 16.3286 9 16.3286 C 7.0574 16.3252 5.1953 15.552 3.8217 14.1783 C 2.448 12.8047 1.6748 10.9426 1.6714 9 C 1.6714 4.9629 4.9629 1.6714 9 1.6714 Z M 9 0 C 4.0371 0 0 4.0371 0 9 C 0 13.9629 4.0371 18 9 18 C 13.9629 18 18 13.9629 18 9 C 18 4.0371 13.9629 0 9 0 Z M 10.2857 3.8571 H 7.7143 V 10.2857 H 10.2857 V 3.8571 Z M 10.2857 11.5714 H 7.7143 V 14.1429 H 10.2857 V 11.5714 Z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">For what it costs to resume a conversation you started before the upgrade, see <a href="#resuming-a-session" class="link">Resuming a session</a>.</span></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="actions-that-keep-the-cache"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#actions-that-keep-the-cache" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Actions that keep the cache</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">These actions either append to the end of the conversation or don’t touch the request at all. Some of them, such as editing CLAUDE.md, keep the cache for the same reason the change doesn’t reach the running session until <code>/clear</code>, <code>/compact</code>, or a restart.</span>
<ul>
<li><a href="#editing-files-in-your-repository" class="link">Editing files in your repository</a></li>
<li><a href="#editing-claude-md-mid-session" class="link">Editing CLAUDE.md mid-session</a></li>
<li><a href="#changing-permission-mode" class="link">Changing permission mode</a></li>
<li><a href="#changing-output-style" class="link">Changing output style</a></li>
<li><a href="#invoking-skills-and-commands" class="link">Invoking skills and commands</a></li>
<li><a href="#running-%2Frecap" class="link">Running <code>/recap</code></a></li>
<li><a href="#rewinding-the-conversation" class="link">Rewinding the conversation</a></li>
<li><a href="#subagents-and-the-cache" class="link">Spawning a subagent</a></li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="editing-files-in-your-repository"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#editing-files-in-your-repository" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Editing files in your repository</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">File contents enter context only when Claude reads them, and reads append to the conversation. Editing a file Claude previously read does not retroactively change the earlier read in history. Instead, Claude Code appends a <code>&lt;system-reminder&gt;</code> noting the file changed, and Claude re-reads it if needed.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="editing-claude-md-mid-session"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#editing-claude-md-mid-session" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Editing CLAUDE.md mid-session</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Your project-root and user-level CLAUDE.md files are read once at session start and held in memory. Editing them mid-session does not invalidate the cache, but the edit also doesn’t apply. Claude keeps working with the version that was loaded at session start. The new content loads on the next <code>/clear</code>, <code>/compact</code>, or restart.</span>
<span data-as="p"><a class="link" href="/docs/en/memory">Nested CLAUDE.md files in subdirectories</a> and <a class="link" href="/docs/en/memory#path-specific-rules">rules with <code>paths:</code> frontmatter</a> load later, when Claude first reads a matching file. Editing one before it loads does take effect. After it loads, the content is part of the conversation history, so a mid-session edit doesn’t retroactively change it.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="changing-permission-mode"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#changing-permission-mode" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Changing permission mode</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Switching between <a class="link" href="/docs/en/permission-modes">permission modes</a>, such as from Manual to accept edits, does not change the system prompt or tool definitions, so mode changes are cache-safe. The exception is plan mode with the <a class="link" href="/docs/en/model-config#opusplan-model-setting"><code>opusplan</code></a> model setting, which switches the model between Opus and Sonnet as you enter or leave plan mode. That makes the mode toggle a <a href="#switching-models" class="link">model switch</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="changing-output-style"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#changing-output-style" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Changing output style</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">When you switch <a class="link" href="/docs/en/output-styles">output styles</a> mid-session with <a class="link" href="/docs/en/output-styles#change-your-output-style"><code>/output-style</code></a>, <code>/config</code>, or the <code>outputStyle</code> setting, Claude uses the new style starting with your next message. Claude Code delivers the new style’s instructions as a message in the conversation, so that request still reads the system prompt and the earlier conversation from the cache.</span>
<span data-as="p">Before v2.1.251, a mid-session style switch kept the cache but didn’t apply until you ran <code>/clear</code> or started a new session.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="invoking-skills-and-commands"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#invoking-skills-and-commands" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Invoking skills and commands</span></h3>
